  const stationCore = EMBEDDED_STATION_CORE;

  const CORE_REGION_ALIASES = {
    "TUV":"TUW",
    "GOVISUMBER":"GOBISUMBER",
    "ORKHON":"ORKHON-UUL",
    "UVURKHANGAI":"UWURKHANGAI",
    "KHUVSGUL":"KHUWSGUL",
    "ZAVKHAN":"ZAWKHAN",
    "KHOVD":"KHOWD",
    "UVS":"UWS"
  };

  function normCoreName(s){
    return String(s ?? "")
      .normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,"")
      .toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g,"");
  }

  function coreRegionKey(displayName){
    if(!displayName || !stationCore?.regions) return null;
    if(stationCore.regions[displayName]) return displayName;
    const upper=String(displayName).toUpperCase();
    if(stationCore.regions[upper]) return upper;
    const alias=CORE_REGION_ALIASES[upper];
    if(alias && stationCore.regions[alias]) return alias;
    const wanted=normCoreName(displayName);
    const aliasNorm=alias ? normCoreName(alias) : wanted;
    return Object.keys(stationCore.regions).find(k=>{
      const nk=normCoreName(k);
      return nk===wanted || nk===aliasNorm;
    }) || null;
  }

  function coreIndex(){
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(243,Number(scrubber.value)||0));
  }

  function coreNationalDaily(){
    return stationCore?.national?.daily_2023_24?.[coreIndex()] || null;
  }

  function coreSelectedDaily(){
    const key=coreRegionKey(selectedRegion);
    if(key) return stationCore?.regions?.[key]?.daily_2023_24?.[coreIndex()] || null;
    return coreNationalDaily();
  }

  function coreSelectedContext(){
    const key=coreRegionKey(selectedRegion);
    if(key) return stationCore?.regions?.[key]?.context || null;
    return stationCore?.national?.context || null;
  }

  function cardinalFromDeg(deg){
    if(deg===null || deg===undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(deg))) return "–";
    const labels=["N","NO","O","SO","S","SW","W","NW"];
    return labels[Math.round((Number(deg)%360)/45)%8];
  }

  function fmtCore(v,digits=1){
    if(v===null || v===undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return "–";
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(currentLocale(),{
      minimumFractionDigits:digits,maximumFractionDigits:digits
    }).format(Number(v));
  }

  function refreshCorePanels(){
    if(!stationCore) return;

    const natDaily=coreNationalDaily();
    const regDaily=coreSelectedDaily();
    const ctx=coreSelectedContext();

    const windEl=document.getElementById("coreWindValue");
    const precipEl=document.getElementById("corePrecipValue");
    if(windEl){
      windEl.textContent=natDaily
        ? `${fmtCore(natDaily.wind_speed_from_daily_mean_components_ms)} m/s`
        : "–";
    }
    if(precipEl){
      precipEl.textContent=natDaily
        ? `${fmtCore(natDaily.total_precipitation_mm_day,1)} mm`
        : "–";
    }

    document.getElementById("contextRegionTitle").textContent=
      selectedRegion || "Mongolei";

    const rw=regDaily?.wind_speed_from_daily_mean_components_ms;
    const rd=regDaily?.wind_direction_from_deg;
    const rp=regDaily?.total_precipitation_mm_day;
    document.getElementById("contextToday").innerHTML=
      `Heute · <strong>Wind ${fmtCore(rw)} m/s ${cardinalFromDeg(rd)}</strong>`+
      ` · Niederschlag ${fmtCore(rp,1)} mm`;

    const elev=ctx?.elevation_m?.mean;
    const precip=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.annual_precipitation_mm?.mean;
    const trange=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.temperature_annual_range_c?.mean;
    const endo=ctx?.hydrology?.endorheic_share_percent;
    const eco=ctx?.ecoregions_2017?.dominant;

    document.getElementById("contextElevation").textContent=
      elev==null ? "–" : `${fmtCore(elev,0)} m`;
    document.getElementById("contextAnnualPrecip").textContent=
      precip==null ? "–" : `${fmtCore(precip,0)} mm`;
    document.getElementById("contextTempRange").textContent=
      trange==null ? "–" : `${fmtCore(trange,1)} °C`;
    document.getElementById("contextEndorheic").textContent=
      endo==null ? "–" : `${fmtCore(endo,0)} %`;
    document.getElementById("contextEcoregion").textContent=eco || "–";
  }


  // ------------------------------------------------------------
  // v9.3 THEMATIC MAP DATA — embedded offline

  function themeLineToPath(line){
    return line.map((p,i)=>{
      const [x,y]=lonLatToXY(p[0],p[1]);
      return `${i===0 ? "M" : "L"} ${x.toFixed(2)} ${y.toFixed(2)}`;
    }).join(" ");
  }

  function themeGeometryToPath(geom){
    if(!geom) return "";
    if(geom.type==="Polygon") return geom.coordinates.map(ringToPath).join(" ");
    if(geom.type==="MultiPolygon") return geom.coordinates.map(poly=>poly.map(ringToPath).join(" ")).join(" ");
    if(geom.type==="LineString") return themeLineToPath(geom.coordinates);
    if(geom.type==="MultiLineString") return geom.coordinates.map(themeLineToPath).join(" ");
    return "";
  }

  function themeHashColor(text){
    const palette=[
      "rgba(128,153,111,.68)","rgba(166,170,111,.68)","rgba(126,163,143,.68)",
      "rgba(182,151,102,.68)","rgba(143,145,105,.68)","rgba(108,144,130,.68)",
      "rgba(190,168,126,.68)","rgba(119,151,151,.68)","rgba(158,135,101,.68)"
    ];
    let h=0;
    for(const ch of String(text||"")) h=((h<<5)-h)+ch.charCodeAt(0);
    return palette[Math.abs(h)%palette.length];
  }

  function themeProperty(feature,names){
    const p=feature?.properties || {};
    for(const n of names){
      if(p[n]!==undefined && p[n]!==null) return p[n];
      const k=Object.keys(p).find(x=>x.toLowerCase()===n.toLowerCase());
      if(k) return p[k];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function drawThematicLayers(){
    const basins=document.getElementById("themeBasins");
    const lakes=document.getElementById("themeLakes");
    const rivers=document.getElementById("themeRivers");
    const eco=document.getElementById("themeEcoregions");

    basins.innerHTML="";
    for(const f of THEME_BASINS.features || []){
      const endo=Number(themeProperty(f,["ENDO"])) || 0;
      basins.appendChild(createSvg("path",{
        d:themeGeometryToPath(f.geometry),
        class:`theme-basin ${endo===1 || endo===2 ? "endo" : ""}`,
        "fill-rule":"evenodd"
      }));
    }

    lakes.innerHTML="";
    for(const f of THEME_LAKES.features || []){
      lakes.appendChild(createSvg("path",{
        d:themeGeometryToPath(f.geometry),class:"theme-lake","fill-rule":"evenodd"
      }));
    }

    rivers.innerHTML="";
    for(const f of THEME_RIVERS.features || []){
      rivers.appendChild(createSvg("path",{
        d:themeGeometryToPath(f.geometry),class:"theme-river"
      }));
    }

    eco.innerHTML="";
    for(const f of THEME_ECOREGIONS.features || []){
      const name=themeProperty(f,["ECO_NAME","ECO_NAME_","NAME"]) || "Ecoregion";
      eco.appendChild(createSvg("path",{
        d:themeGeometryToPath(f.geometry),
        class:"theme-eco",
        fill:themeHashColor(name),
        "fill-rule":"evenodd"
      }));
    }

    updateWindLayer();
  }

  function updateWindLayer(){
    // v9.4.1: legacy 22-arrow SVG layer intentionally disabled.
    const layer=document.getElementById("themeWind");
    if(layer) layer.innerHTML="";
  }

  function refreshThemeMetric(){
    const el=document.getElementById("layerMetric");
    if(!el) return;
    el.textContent="";
    const ctx=coreSelectedContext();

    if(activeTheme==="relief"){
      const v=ctx?.elevation_m?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`Gewählter Raum: mittlere Höhe ${fmtCore(v,0)} m`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="moisture"){
      const v=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.annual_precipitation_mm?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`Gewählter Raum: ${fmtCore(v,0)} mm Jahresniederschlag`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="temperature"){
      const v=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.temperature_annual_range_c?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`Gewählter Raum: ${fmtCore(v,1)} °C jährliche Temperaturspanne`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="wind"){
      const d=coreSelectedDaily();
      if(d) el.textContent=`Gewählter Raum: ${fmtCore(d.wind_speed_from_daily_mean_components_ms,1)} m/s · aus ${cardinalFromDeg(d.wind_direction_from_deg)}`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="water"){
      const v=ctx?.hydrology?.endorheic_share_percent;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`Gewählter Raum: ${fmtCore(v,0)} % endorheischer Beckenanteil`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="vegetation"){
      const eco=ctx?.ecoregions_2017?.dominant;
      const key=coreRegionKey(selectedRegion);
      const ndvi=key ? THEME_PREWINTER_NDVI[String(key).toUpperCase()] : null;
      const parts=[];
      if(eco) parts.push(`dominant: ${eco}`);
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(ndvi))) parts.push(`Vorwinter-NDVI: ${fmtCore(ndvi,2)}`);
      el.textContent=parts.join(" · ");
    }
  }

  function hideAllThemes(){
    for(const id of [
      "themeRelief","themeMoisture","themeTempRange",
      "themeBasins","themeEcoregions","themeLakes","themeRivers","themeWind"
    ]){
      const el=document.getElementById(id);
      if(el) el.style.display="none";
    }
  }

  function setTheme(theme){
    activeTheme=theme;
    hideAllThemes();

    const veil=document.getElementById("mapVeil");
    const staticTheme=["relief","moisture","temperature","water","vegetation"].includes(theme);

    stage.classList.toggle("theme-static",staticTheme);
    stage.classList.toggle("theme-wind",theme==="wind");
    scrubber.disabled=staticTheme;

    document.querySelectorAll(".layer-btn[data-theme]").forEach(btn=>{
      btn.classList.toggle("active",btn.dataset.theme===theme);
    });

    if(theme==="winter"){
      veil.style.display="none";
      veil.setAttribute("opacity","0");
      if(!dragging) scheduleIdle();
    } else {
      stopIdle();
      video.pause();
      veil.style.display="";
      veil.setAttribute("opacity","0.96");
    }

    if(theme==="relief") document.getElementById("themeRelief").style.display="";
    if(theme==="moisture") document.getElementById("themeMoisture").style.display="";
    if(theme==="temperature") document.getElementById("themeTempRange").style.display="";
    if(theme==="wind"){
      document.getElementById("themeWind").style.display="";
      updateWindLayer();
    }
    if(theme==="water"){
      document.getElementById("themeBasins").style.display="";
      document.getElementById("themeLakes").style.display="";
      document.getElementById("themeRivers").style.display="";
    }
    if(theme==="vegetation") document.getElementById("themeEcoregions").style.display="";

    const meta=THEME_META[theme] || THEME_META.winter;
    document.getElementById("layerKicker").textContent=meta.kicker;
    document.getElementById("layerTitle").textContent=meta.title;
    document.getElementById("layerSub").textContent=meta.sub;
    refreshThemeMetric();

    stage.dataset.layersReady="1";
    stage.dataset.activeTheme=theme;
  }

  function initThemeButtons(){
    document.querySelectorAll(".layer-btn[data-theme]").forEach(btn=>{
      btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>e.stopPropagation());
      btn.addEventListener("click",e=>{
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        setTheme(btn.dataset.theme);
      });
    });
  }

  function currentLocale(){ return I18N[currentLang]?.locale || "de-DE"; }
  function L(){ return I18N[currentLang] || I18N.de; }

  function setText(selector,text){
    const el=typeof selector==="string" ? document.querySelector(selector) : selector;
    if(el) el.textContent=text;
  }

  function setLabelFor(valueId,text){
    const v=document.getElementById(valueId);
    const label=v?.closest(".climate-item,.context-item")?.querySelector(".label");
    if(label) label.textContent=text;
  }

  function translatedCardinal(deg){
    if(deg===null || deg===undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(deg))) return "–";
    const idx=Math.round((Number(deg)%360)/45)%8;
    if(currentLang==="mn") return ["хойд","зүүн хойд","зүүн","зүүн өмнөд","өмнөд","баруун өмнөд","баруун","баруун хойд"][idx];
    if(currentLang==="en") return ["N","NE","E","SE","S","SW","W","NW"][idx];
    return ["N","NO","O","SO","S","SW","W","NW"][idx];
  }

  function formatDateForLang(d){
    try{
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(currentLocale(),{
        day:"2-digit",month:"2-digit",year:"numeric",timeZone:"UTC"
      }).format(d);
    }catch(_){ return isoDateUTC(d); }
  }

  function applyStaticI18n(){
    const t=L();
    document.documentElement.lang=currentLang==="mn" ? "mn" : currentLang;

    document.querySelectorAll(".lang-btn").forEach(btn=>{
      btn.classList.toggle("active",btn.dataset.lang===currentLang);
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed",btn.dataset.lang===currentLang ? "true" : "false");
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".layer-btn[data-theme]").forEach(btn=>{
      const x=t.layerButtons[btn.dataset.theme];
      if(x) btn.textContent=x;
    });
    const geology=document.querySelector(".layer-btn:disabled");
    if(geology) geology.textContent=t.layerButtons.geology;

    const labels=document.querySelectorAll("#context .context-label");
    if(labels[0]) labels[0].textContent=t.countryRussia;
    if(labels[1]) labels[1].textContent=t.countryChina;
    setText("#ubLabel",t.ub);

    setText("#climatePanel .eyebrow",t.climateHeading);
    setText("#climatePanel .climate-unit",t.dailyMean);
    setLabelFor("tempMin",t.minimum);
    setLabelFor("tempMax",t.maximum);
    setLabelFor("sweValue",t.swe);
    setLabelFor("densityValue",t.snowDensity);
    setLabelFor("snowDepthValue",t.snowDepth);
    setLabelFor("coreWindValue",t.wind);
    setLabelFor("corePrecipValue",t.precip);

    setText("#contextPanel .eyebrow",t.spatialContext);
    setLabelFor("contextElevation",t.elevation);
    setLabelFor("contextAnnualPrecip",t.annualPrecip);
    setLabelFor("contextTempRange",t.tempRange);
    setLabelFor("contextEndorheic",t.endorheic);
    setLabelFor("contextEcoregion",t.ecoregion);
    setText("#contextSource",t.sourceContext);

    setText("#infoPanel .empty-content .eyebrow",t.emptyEyebrow);
    setText("#infoPanel .empty-content h1",t.chooseAimag);
    setText("#infoPanel .empty-content p",t.chooseText);
    setText("#infoPanel .data-content > .eyebrow",t.regionEyebrow);

    const kpis=document.querySelectorAll("#migrationMetric .kpi-label");
    [t.per1000,t.population,t.inMigration,t.outMigration].forEach((x,i)=>{
      if(kpis[i]) kpis[i].textContent=x;
    });

    setText("#infoPanel .warning",t.warning);
    setText("#hint",t.timelineHint);
    setText("#timelineCover div",t.timelineStatic);

    const map=document.getElementById("mapOverlay");
    if(map) map.setAttribute("aria-label",t.ariaMap);
    scrubber.setAttribute("aria-label",t.ariaTimeline);
    const ms=document.getElementById("migrationSparkline");
    if(ms) ms.setAttribute("aria-label",t.ariaMigration);

    const meta=t.themes[activeTheme] || t.themes.winter;
    setText("#layerKicker",meta[0]);
    setText("#layerTitle",meta[1]);
    setText("#layerSub",meta[2]);
    updateI18nTimeline();
  }

  function applyDynamicI18n(){
    const t=L();
    const d=selectedDate();
    setText("#climateDate",formatDateForLang(d));

    const meta=climateData?.meta || {};
    const ca=document.getElementById("climateAvailability");
    if(ca) ca.textContent=t.climateAvail(meta.files_found ?? "?",meta.expected_files ?? 40);
    setText("#coreDailyAvailability",t.coreAvail);

    const regDaily=coreSelectedDaily();
    const rw=regDaily?.wind_speed_from_daily_mean_components_ms;
    const rd=regDaily?.wind_direction_from_deg;
    const rp=regDaily?.total_precipitation_mm_day;
    setText("#contextRegionTitle",selectedRegion || (currentLang==="mn" ? "Монгол" : "Mongolia"));
    const today=document.getElementById("contextToday");
    if(today){
      today.innerHTML=
        `${t.today} · <strong>${t.wind} ${fmtCore(rw)} m/s ${translatedCardinal(rd)}</strong>`+
        ` · ${t.precip} ${fmtCore(rp,1)} mm`;
    }

    if(selectedRegion && stationData?.regions){
      const r=stationData.regions[selectedRegion];
      if(r){
        const year=livestockYearForDate(d);
        const period=periodForDate(d);
        const latinName=selectedRegion;
        const mongolianName=r.mongolian_name || "";

        setText("#regionName",currentLang==="mn" ? (mongolianName || latinName) : latinName);
        setText("#regionNameMn",currentLang==="mn" ? latinName : mongolianName);

        const livestock=r.livestock?.[year] || {};
        setText("#livestockTitle",`${t.livestock} ${year}`);
        makeBars(
          document.getElementById("livestockBars"),
          ["Horse","Cattle","Camel","Sheep","Goat"].map(key=>({label:t.species[key],value:livestock[key]}))
        );
        const firstSub=document.querySelector("#infoPanel .data-content > .metric .metric-sub");
        if(firstSub) firstSub.textContent=t.livestockSub;

        const losses=r.animal_losses?.[period] || {};
        setText("#lossTitle",t.losses);
        makeBars(
          document.getElementById("lossBars"),
          ["Horse","Cattle","Camel","Sheep","Goat"].map(key=>({label:t.species[key],value:losses[key]}))
        );
        const qLabel={"2023-4":"Q4 2023","2024-1":"Q1 2024","2024-2":"Q2 2024"}[period] || period;
        setText("#lossPeriod",`NSO · ${qLabel} · ${t.livestockSub.replace(/^NSO · /,"")}`);

        const y=String(d.getUTCFullYear()===2023 ? 2023 : 2024);
        setText("#migrationTitle",`${t.migration} ${y}`);
        setText("#migrationSub",t.migrationSub);
      }
    }

    refreshThemeMetricI18n();
    updateI18nTimeline();
  }

  function refreshThemeMetricI18n(){
    const t=L();
    const el=document.getElementById("layerMetric");
    if(!el) return;
    el.textContent="";
    const ctx=coreSelectedContext();

    if(activeTheme==="relief"){
      const v=ctx?.elevation_m?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`${t.selected}: ${t.meanElevation} ${fmtCore(v,0)} m`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="moisture"){
      const v=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.annual_precipitation_mm?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`${t.selected}: ${fmtCore(v,0)} mm ${t.annualPrecipShort}`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="temperature"){
      const v=ctx?.worldclim_1970_2000?.temperature_annual_range_c?.mean;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`${t.selected}: ${fmtCore(v,1)} °C ${t.annualTempRangeShort}`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="wind"){
      const row=coreSelectedDaily();
      if(row) el.textContent=`${t.selected}: ${fmtCore(row.wind_speed_from_daily_mean_components_ms,1)} m/s · ${t.from} ${translatedCardinal(row.wind_direction_from_deg)}`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="water"){
      const v=ctx?.hydrology?.endorheic_share_percent;
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(v))) el.textContent=`${t.selected}: ${fmtCore(v,0)} % ${t.endorheicShare}`;
    }
    if(activeTheme==="vegetation"){
      const eco=ctx?.ecoregions_2017?.dominant;
      const key=coreRegionKey(selectedRegion);
      const ndvi=key ? THEME_PREWINTER_NDVI[String(key).toUpperCase()] : null;
      const parts=[];
      if(eco) parts.push(`${t.dominant}: ${eco}`);
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(ndvi))) parts.push(`${t.prewinterNdvi}: ${fmtCore(ndvi,2)}`);
      el.textContent=parts.join(" · ");
    }
  }

  function updateI18nTimeline(){
    const timeline=document.getElementById("i18nTimeline");
    if(!timeline) return;
    const t=L();
    const d=selectedDate();
    const idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(243,Number(scrubber.value)||0));
    const ratio=idx/243;
    const x=8.59375 + ratio*82.8125;

    setText("#i18nTimelineTitle",activeTheme==="wind" ? t.timelineWind : t.timelineWinter);
    setText("#i18nTimelineDate",formatDateForLang(d));

    const dateEl=document.getElementById("i18nTimelineDate");
    const dot=document.getElementById("i18nTimelineDot");
    if(dateEl) dateEl.style.left=`${x}%`;
    if(dot) dot.style.left=`${x}%`;

    const ticks=document.getElementById("i18nTimelineTicks");
    if(ticks){
      const starts=[0,31,61,92,123,152,183,213];
      ticks.innerHTML="";
      starts.forEach((day,i)=>{
        const e=document.createElement("div");
        e.className=`i18n-month ${i===0 ? "first" : ""}`;
        e.style.left=`${day/243*100}%`;
        e.textContent=t.months[i];
        ticks.appendChild(e);
      });
    }
  }

  function initLanguageSwitch(){
    document.querySelectorAll(".lang-btn").forEach(btn=>{
      btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>e.stopPropagation());
      btn.addEventListener("click",e=>{
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        const lang=btn.dataset.lang;
        if(!LANGS.includes(lang)) return;
        currentLang=lang;
        try{ localStorage.setItem("dzud-lang",lang); }catch(_){}
        applyStaticI18n();
        if(climateData) refreshClimatePanel();
        if(stationCore) refreshCorePanels();
        if(selectedRegion) refreshRegionPanel();
        refreshThemeMetricI18n();
        applyDynamicI18n();
      });
    });
    applyStaticI18n();
  }

  const __origRefreshClimatePanel=refreshClimatePanel;
  refreshClimatePanel=function(){
    const r=__origRefreshClimatePanel.apply(this,arguments);
    applyDynamicI18n();
    return r;
  };

  const __origRefreshCorePanels=refreshCorePanels;
  refreshCorePanels=function(){
    const r=__origRefreshCorePanels.apply(this,arguments);
    applyDynamicI18n();
    return r;
  };

  const __origRefreshRegionPanel=refreshRegionPanel;
  refreshRegionPanel=function(){
    const r=__origRefreshRegionPanel.apply(this,arguments);
    applyDynamicI18n();
    return r;
  };

  const __origSetTheme=setTheme;
  setTheme=function(theme){
    const r=__origSetTheme.apply(this,arguments);
    applyStaticI18n();
    applyDynamicI18n();
    return r;
  };

  function loadData(){
    try{
      geojson = EMBEDDED_GEOJSON;
      stationData = EMBEDDED_STATION_DATA;

      if(!geojson || !Array.isArray(geojson.features)){
        throw new Error("Embedded GeoJSON is missing or invalid.");
      }
      if(!stationData){
        throw new Error("Embedded regional station data is missing.");
      }
      if(!stationCore || stationCore?.dates?.length !== 244 ||
         Object.keys(stationCore?.regions || {}).length !== 22){
        throw new Error("Embedded station core is missing or invalid.");
      }

      drawAimags();
      drawThematicLayers();
      initThemeButtons();
      initLanguageSwitch();
      setTheme("winter");
      applyStaticI18n();
      applyDynamicI18n();

      climateData = EMBEDDED_CLIMATE_DATA || {meta:{},days:[]};
      climateByDate = new Map(
        (climateData.days || []).map(d => [d.date, d])
      );
      refreshClimatePanel();
      refreshCorePanels();
      if(activeTheme==="wind") updateWindLayer();
      refreshThemeMetric();

      stage.dataset.coreReady = "1";
      stage.dataset.coreVersion = "v9.2.1";
      errorBox.classList.remove("show");
      errorBox.innerHTML = "";
    }catch(err){
      errorBox.classList.add("show");
      errorBox.innerHTML =
        "<b>Eingebettete Daten konnten nicht initialisiert werden.</b><br>" +
        "Fehler: " + String(err);
    }
  }

  function drawAimags(){
    aimagLayer.innerHTML = "";

    for(const feature of geojson.features){
      const name = feature.properties.NSO_NAME;
      const path = createSvg("path",{
        "class":"aimag",
        "d":geometryToPath(feature.geometry),
        "data-region":name,
        "fill-rule":"evenodd"
      });

      path.addEventListener("pointerdown", e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        selectRegion(name);
      });

      aimagLayer.appendChild(path);
    }

    // tatsächliche UB-Position
    const [ux,uy] = lonLatToXY(106.9057,47.8864);
    const dot = document.getElementById("ubDot");
    const label = document.getElementById("ubLabel");
    dot.setAttribute("cx",ux); dot.setAttribute("cy",uy);
    label.setAttribute("x",ux+18); label.setAttribute("y",uy+9);
  }

  function selectRegion(name){
    selectedRegion = name;

    document.querySelectorAll(".aimag").forEach(el=>{
      el.classList.toggle("selected", el.dataset.region === name);
    });

    infoPanel.classList.remove("empty");
    refreshRegionPanel();
    refreshCorePanels();
      if(activeTheme==="wind") updateWindLayer();
      refreshThemeMetric();
  }

  function selectedDate(){
    const d = new Date(START_DATE);
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + Number(scrubber.value));
    return d;
  }

  function isoDateUTC(d){
    return d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  }

  function fmtClimate(v, digits=1){
    if(v===null || v===undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(v))) return "–";
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(currentLocale(),{
      minimumFractionDigits:digits,
      maximumFractionDigits:digits
    }).format(Number(v));
  }

  function refreshClimatePanel(){
    const panel = document.getElementById("climatePanel");
    const d = selectedDate();
    const iso = isoDateUTC(d);
    const row = climateByDate.get(iso);

    document.getElementById("climateDate").textContent =
      d.toLocaleDateString("de-DE",{
        day:"2-digit", month:"long", year:"numeric", timeZone:"UTC"
      });

    if(!row || row.t2m_mean===null || row.t2m_mean===undefined){
      panel.classList.add("missing");
      document.getElementById("tempMean").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("tempMin").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("tempMax").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("sweValue").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("densityValue").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("snowDepthValue").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("climateAvailability").textContent =
        "Für diesen Tag sind die ERA5-Daten noch nicht heruntergeladen.";
      return;
    }

    panel.classList.remove("missing");
    document.getElementById("tempMean").textContent =
      `${fmtClimate(row.t2m_mean)}°`;
    document.getElementById("tempMin").textContent =
      row.t2m_min == null ? "–" : `${fmtClimate(row.t2m_min)} °C`;
    document.getElementById("tempMax").textContent =
      row.t2m_max == null ? "–" : `${fmtClimate(row.t2m_max)} °C`;
    document.getElementById("sweValue").textContent =
      row.swe_mm == null ? "–" : `${fmtClimate(row.swe_mm)} mm`;
    document.getElementById("densityValue").textContent =
      row.snow_density_mean == null ? "–" : `${fmtClimate(row.snow_density_mean,0)} kg/m³`;
    document.getElementById("snowDepthValue").textContent =
      row.derived_snow_depth_cm == null ? "–" : `${fmtClimate(row.derived_snow_depth_cm)} cm`;

    const meta = climateData?.meta || {};
    document.getElementById("climateAvailability").textContent =
      `Mongolei-Flächenmittel · ${meta.files_found ?? "?"}/${meta.expected_files ?? 40} ERA5-Dateien vorhanden · *Schneehöhe aus SWE + Dichte abgeleitet`;
  }

  function periodForDate(d){
    const y = d.getUTCFullYear();
    const m = d.getUTCMonth()+1;
    if(y===2023) return "2023-4";
    if(y===2024 && m<=3) return "2024-1";
    return "2024-2";
  }

  function livestockYearForDate(d){
    return d.getUTCFullYear() === 2023 ? "2023" : "2024";
  }

  function fmtK(v){
    if(v===null || v===undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(v))) return "–";
    const n = Number(v);
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(currentLocale(),{maximumFractionDigits:1}).format(n);
  }

  function makeBars(container, values){
    container.innerHTML = "";
    const max = Math.max(...values.map(x=>Number(x.value)||0),1);

    for(const item of values){
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "species";

      const name = document.createElement("div");
      name.className = "species-name";
      name.textContent = item.label;

      const bar = document.createElement("div");
      bar.className = "bar";
      const fill = document.createElement("span");
      fill.style.width = `${Math.max(0,(Number(item.value)||0)/max*100)}%`;
      bar.appendChild(fill);

      const val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "species-value";
      val.textContent = fmtK(item.value);

      row.append(name,bar,val);
      container.appendChild(row);
    }
  }



  function fmtInt(v){
    if(v===null || v===undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(v))) return "–";
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(currentLocale(),{maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(Number(v));
  }

  function fmtSignedInt(v){
    if(v===null || v===undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(v))) return "–";
    const n = Number(v);
    return `${n>0?"+":""}${fmtInt(n)}`;
  }

  function refreshMigrationPanel(){
    if(!selectedRegion || !migrationData?.regions) return;
    const series = migrationData.regions[selectedRegion];
    if(!series) return;

    const d = selectedDate();
    const year = String(d.getUTCFullYear()===2023 ? 2023 : 2024);
    const row = series[year] || null;

    const netEl = document.getElementById("migrationNet");
    document.getElementById("migrationTitle").textContent = `Binnenmigration ${year}`;

    if(!row){
      netEl.textContent = "–";
      netEl.classList.remove("positive","negative");
      document.getElementById("migrationRate").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("migrationPopulation").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("migrationIn").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("migrationOut").textContent = "–";
      document.getElementById("migrationSparkline").innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }

    const net = Number(row.net_migration);
    netEl.textContent = fmtSignedInt(net);
    netEl.classList.toggle("positive", net>0);
    netEl.classList.toggle("negative", net<0);
    document.getElementById("migrationRate").textContent =
      row.net_migration_per_1000==null ? "–" : `${row.net_migration_per_1000>0?"+":""}${fmtClimate(row.net_migration_per_1000,1)}`;
    document.getElementById("migrationPopulation").textContent = fmtInt(row.population);
    document.getElementById("migrationIn").textContent = fmtInt(row.in_migration);
    document.getElementById("migrationOut").textContent = fmtInt(row.out_migration);

    drawMigrationSparkline(series, Number(year));
    document.getElementById("migrationSub").textContent =
      "NSO · jährlicher Langzeitkontext 2000–2025 · keine direkte Dzud-Kausalität";
  }

  function drawMigrationSparkline(series, selectedYear){
    const svg = document.getElementById("migrationSparkline");
    svg.innerHTML = "";
    const rows = Object.entries(series)
      .map(([year,r])=>({year:Number(year),value:Number(r?.net_migration)}))
      .filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.value))
      .sort((a,b)=>a.year-b.year);
    if(rows.length<2) return;

    const W=260,H=62,L=3,R=3,T=5,B=12;
    const minY=Math.min(0,...rows.map(x=>x.value));
    const maxY=Math.max(0,...rows.map(x=>x.value));
    const span=Math.max(1,maxY-minY);
    const xFor=(i)=>L+i*(W-L-R)/(rows.length-1);
    const yFor=(v)=>T+(maxY-v)/span*(H-T-B);

    const zero=document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg","line");
